| Yeti, Flink's `Yeti` (0x30044), MOUNTAIN's: 12 in the converted maps (18-1 three, 18-3 one, 19-3 one, 20-1 five, 22-1 one, 22-2's side room one, spawned by a Generator) | `Muta Yeti n`: object 74 | Its records `STAY` (asleep), `WARN`, `SPIN`, `WAIT` (tired) and `DEAD`, with Flink's hitboxes, and MAIN.WLD's clods `ERDE` (its dust, as the Puge's). Param: facing left, always 0 (Flink's placements have no params and face right). |

No params (Flink's init reads none). |
| Storm Cloud (0x231B2), META's dark cloud: 7 in the converted maps (29-1 two, 30-1 two, 30-2 three), 2 of them spawned by Generators; each map's first placement (spawn flag bit 6: it only builds the shrunk frames) is left out | `Muta Storm n`: object 74 | `WOLK` with its mouth's selector 1 kept; `WIND`, the puffs, one sprite a frame at 0, 0 (Flink draws its hardware sprite with its top-left at the puff); `BOOM` (`DEAD`, broken up). No params (Flink's init reads none). |
| Blitz dude (0x22DA2), META's man on a cloud: 5 in the converted maps (29-1 two, 30-2 three), 2 of them spawned by Generators | `Muta Blitz n`: object 74 | Its records `STAY`, `DEAD` and `ZAP ` (the lightning, its hitbox the strike's), with Flink's hitboxes, and `WOLK` and `WARN` (the cloud) with a platform's standing surface (widened by `PLATFORM_EDGE`), for the cloud let go. The converter checks that `STAY`'s cloud attach point is at 0, 8 (`BLITZ_CLOUD_AT`). No params (Flink's init reads none). |

| Razorback (0x27486), MOUNTAIN's stampede of Razorbacks: 4 in the converted maps, all in 21-1 (x 536, 910 and 1524 from the left, 2128 from the right) | `Muta Razo n`: object 74 | Its records `RUN ` and `DEAD`, with Flink's hitboxes. The placement is an unseen spawner; the Razorbacks are its children. Params: from the right (Flink's `move left` box), `time` (the frames it sends Razorbacks for: 400, 700, 550, 2000). |
| Cyclin' Dude (0x3045A), Chopper Bike (0x30724) and Little Dude (0x3099C), VILLAGE: one Cyclin' Dude in each of 9-1, 10-2, 10-4, 11-1, 11-2 (all spawned by a Generator); 19 Little Dudes (14 of them in 13-2's side room, spawned by Generators) | `Muta Cycl n`, `Muta Bike n`, `Muta Litt n`: object 74 | The rider's `HANG`, `DEAD`, `WALK`, `SL45`; the bike's `CYCL`, `DEAD`; the Little Dude's `WALK`, `STUN` (with its effects), `DEAD` and MAIN.WLD's `Shot` `SMAL` as `SHSM` (it is small). Flink spawns the bike and the Little Dudes by type name (0x32BD4), so their maps place one of each never to spawn (spawn flag bit 5), only to load the type; they are never-spawned placements (0x2000) here too, and FLINK.DLL finds the types by name (`type_named`). The rider's params: facing left, then his `HANG` hooks 1 (the bike, 7, −8) and 2 (the drop point, −5, −7) (the converter's `cycl_params`). |
| Firemonster (0x2F226), FOREST: 14 in the converted maps, 3 in level 1-2 | `Muta Fire n`: object 74 | Its `WALK` record; its flames `FIRE` and `FADE` and MAIN.WLD's smoke `DUST`, one sprite a frame at (0, 0); `BOOM` (`WALK`'s second frame) to shatter. Params: facing left, and the mouth's offset (29, −25), the attach point with flags 6 in `WALK`'s second frame. |

Params: facing left, and its hand's offset (16, −10), the attach point with flags 1 in `WALK`'s first frame. |

| The Forest Boss, the Forest Boss, `Boss` (0x2948C, its FOREST branch 0x294B0): 1, in 8-2 (FOREST map 17). VILLAGE's `Boss` is the second (below), META's another, not converted | `Muta Boss n`: object 74 | `WALK`, `JUMP` and `OUCH` with its stilts' spiked tops (`NODE`) drawn in at the frames' attach points with hook 1, 11 px left of them, the left one mirrored; `LGWK`, `LGJP`, `LGOU`: those two points per frame (blank pieces); `DEAD`, `BOOM` (`DEAD`'s frame 0) and MAIN.WLD's `SPIR` and `TAIL` (the Spirit). Params: 0, the fight's music (Flink's index 8, `TRACK114`) and its defeat's (index 21, `TRACK110`) as Lomax Music params. Its placement links (flag 0x8000) to the arena's Finish, which never spawns by proximity (0x2000). |
| The Village Boss, `Boss` (0x2948C, its VILLAGE branch 0x29A9E): 1, in 16-2 (VILLAGE map 17). META's `Boss` is another one, not converted | `Muta Village n`: object 74 | `RUN `, `WALK`, `STAY` (the Village Boss; their attach points left out); `PRUN`, `PWAL`, `PSTA`: each frame's three men's points (hooks 1 and 2, blank pieces in the frame's order); `MEN1` (the middle man), `MEN2` (hit once), `MEN3` (dead), `MEN4` (a side man), `MEN5` (dead); `BAL1` (a chain link), `BAL3` (the middle chain's spiked head), `BL2L` and `BL2R` (the side chains' hands, `BAL2` flipped upside down, the right one both ways), each one sprite with its top-left at the point, as Flink's `Sprite_DrawScreen` draws them; `DEAD`; `BOOM` (`DEAD`'s body, its first 17 pieces) and `BOO2` (the 18th: Lomax's `Actor_ShatterIntoPieces` stores 8 bytes a piece from actor +0x94, so 17 fit; an 18th overwrote the next actor's type and the game crashed). Params: 0, the music at its defeat (index 21, `TRACK110`; the fight's is the map's, index 9). Its placement links (0x8000) to the arena's Finish (0x2000). The map's never-spawned `Stone` and `Jumppad` placements (Flink's spawn flag bit 5, only to load the types) are `Spik Stone` and `Mega Jumppad` placements that FLINK.DLL spawns where Flink spawns them by name (0x32BD4). |
| The Meta Boss, `Boss` (0x2948C, its META branch 0x2A540): 1, in 31-2 (META map 6), META's boss | `Muta MetaBoss n`: object 74 | `EINS` (defeated), `ZWEI` (standing), `DREI` (pounding the ground, 12 frames) and `VIER` (clapping, 24 frames), each with only its arms and hands (the pieces with selector 1, which Flink's draw 0x2ACB8 gives the sprite priority bit; `EINS` has none: a blank piece), and `BEIN`, `BZWE`, `BDRE`, `BVIE` with the same frames' body and head (the other pieces); `ZAP1` (the lightning charging), `ZAP2` (the ball), `ZAP3` (the strike); `WOLK` (its clouds) with a platform's standing surface. The converter checks the eyes, the frames' two attach points with hook 1, at (3, −79) and (24, −79) (`MBOSS_EYES`), `VIER`'s second box, the clap's (`MBOSS_CLAP_BOX`), and that `ZWEI`'s arms break up into at most 17 pieces. Params: 0, the fight's music (index 8, `TRACK114`) and the music at its defeat (index 21, `TRACK110`). Its placement links (0x8000) to the arena's Finish (0x2000). |
| The Donkey monkey, `Donkey monkey` (0x220FC): 1, in 28-1 (TREE map 7), TREE's boss | `Muta Donk n`: object 74 | `STAY` (its two hide-selector pieces kept), `JUMP` (5 frames), `LAND`, `OUCH` (its attach point, hook 7 = Hook_Nop, left out). Params: 0, the fight's music (index 9, `TRACK113`) and the music at its defeat (index 21, `TRACK110`). Its placement links (0x8000) to the arena's Finish (0x2000). The converter also writes a `Spik Apple` (`distance` 1000) and a `Spik App2` (start speed 0) at its x, y 0x3C, never spawned by proximity (0x2000): the apples FLINK.DLL spawns where Flink spawns them by name (0x32BD4). They are not counted among the map's Falling apples and App2s. |
| The final boss, `Bos1` (0x20E86): 1, in 24-2 (MOUNTAIN map 17). The map's `Bos2` (0x20DCC, which builds the dragon's sprites) and `Boom` (0x216EA) are only his | `Muta Bos1 n`: object 74 | `STAY`, `STA2`, `FLY `, `OUCH`, `ZAP ` (the sorcerer forming out of his fireball), `WARN` and `HEAD` (his fireballs, and himself as a fireball), `TAIL` (their sparks); `Bos2`'s `HEAD` and `TAIL` as `DHED` and `DTAL` (the dragon); `Boom`'s `HEAD`, `TAIL` and `BOOM` as `BHED`, `BTAL` and `BOOM`. The `Bos2` and `Boom` placements are left out. The converter checks `STA2`'s point 1 (8, −39), where his fireballs come from (`BOS1_FIRE_AT`), `FLY`'s point 2 (−2, −32), where his Bos3 dudes drop from (`BOS1_DROP_AT`), `OUCH`'s top-left (−21, −61) (`BOS1_SHRINK_AT`) and the four one-sprite records FLINK.DLL places by Flink's offsets (`BOS1_PIECES`). Params: 0, the music at his defeat (index 21, `TRACK110`; the fight's is the map's, index 8). His placement links (0x8000) to a Finish in the middle of the map (0x2000). |
| The final boss's Bos3 dudes, `Bos3 dude` (0x21F90): placed once in 24-2, never spawned (spawn flag bit 5: it loads the type) | `Muta Bos3 n`: object 74 | `MOVE`, `STUN` (with the stun effect's records, as the Forest Dummy's) and `DEAD`. No params. |

**Forest Dummy** (Flink's think 0x2F9A0 and walker 0x1D0C4, at 60 frames a second as Lomax's ticks):
- **Walking.** It speeds up by 0x1000 a frame to 0.5 px a frame. A wall at x ± 8 turns it at once. With no ground ahead (none within 4 cells below x ± 8) it stops and turns, so it never walks off a ledge. It keeps to the ground under it (within 3 px at that speed) and turns back where there is none. Down a 45° slope it slides (`SL45`, up to 1.5 px a frame); it won't walk up one.
- **Touching it** hurts Lomax: Lomax's own contact damage (the helmet comes off, or he dies without it).
- **Stomping it** (Lomax's own stomp: falling onto it from above bounces him off) stuns it for 300 frames (`STUN`). It pops up 3.0 px a frame and falls back, and is harmless while stunned. Then it hops up again and walks on.
- **The stun effect.** Over a stunned enemy Flink draws bubbles, and for the last 120 frames red wake-up lines instead. Its `STUN` frames carry an attach point whose hook (0x1B4BC) draws one frame of MAIN.WLD's `Shot` type there: `STUN` (4 frames, speed 35) or `WAKE` (2 frames, speed 42), its top-left 8 px left of and 16 px above the point, each on a counter of its own. Lomax draws one record at a time, so the converter gives the enemy `STUN` and `WAKE` records with a frame for each pair of its own frame and an effect frame. FLINK.DLL picks the frame each tick from the three counters (`stun_frame`).
- **Lomax's attacks** (spin, tumble, the thrown helmet) count as a stomp, as Lomax's zombies take two hits of any kind (in Flink its slide and spells kill it at once): the first stuns it, with the hit sound its death plays (0x59, `BOUNCE2`, as a zombie's first hit; a stomp has Lomax's bounce sound), the next kills it. Lomax's attack check (`Actor_CheckHitByPlayer` 0x413E40) reports a hit on every tick that a spin or tumble overlaps the enemy, so `enemy_attacked` (enemy.c) counts only the first tick of an overlap, and none in the 30 ticks after a first hit; `Enemy_ZombieRunner_Update` (0x42E220) makes a zombie unhittable for 90 ticks after its first hit for the same reason. The enemy stays hittable, so a stomp still kills it at once.
- **Rolling down a steep slope** into it kills it in one hit, as Flink's slide does. Lomax's roll is his `TUMB` anim, which `Player_Update` sets (0x411C5A) while the slope-slide state bit 8 is on (0x41078A); `enemy_attacked` returns 2 for a hit in `TUMB` (not the helmet's), at any tick and during the guard. The same goes for the Ingredient Holder, except while it blinks after its first hit (nothing touches it then).
- **Picking it up.** While stunned it can be picked up and thrown (see Grabbing and throwing). Its stun runs on while it is carried; when it ends the dummy jumps out of Lomax's hands and hurts him.
- **Stomping it while stunned**, or attacking it, kills it (an attack throws it away from Lomax). It shows `DEAD`, is thrown up at 4.0 px a frame, falls through the map at 0.25 px a frame per frame (at most 4.0) and is removed.
- **Dead for good.** Flink's `Enemy_Die` (0x1B12E) forgets the enemy's map entry (0x1B13A clears its bit in the saved pending bits at 0x6480), so it never spawns again in that level: not after a lost life, and not when its map is entered again through a door. FLINK.DLL does the same through the memory it keeps for opened chests (`forget_placement`, see Gone for good).
- **A coin.** A dying enemy drops a Lomax coin (`Item_SpawnCoin` 0x42C240, the `Items` type of Lomax's MAIN.WLD), thrown up as `Zombie_FreeLemming` (0x42CD00) throws it. Flink's enemies drop nothing. As with Lomax's zombies, only when its x is odd, so about half the time.
- **The defeated count.** Each dying enemy adds one to the HUD's lemming count (`g_LemmingsFreed` 0x52C5A8) and makes it flash for 60 ticks (`g_HudLemmingFlashTimer` 0x52C700), as `Zombie_FreeLemming` does for a zombie, without the freed lemming that walks away, and sets a spirit free (see Spirits). Unlike Lomax's zombies, 50 don't open the bonus trapdoor; the count works as the coins do: the 100th defeated enemy gives a 1UP and the count starts again from 0. The 1UP is Lomax's own, as `Item_UpdateCoinOrHelmet` (0x42C809) makes it for 100 coins: `g_bPending1Up` (0x59E770) and the `Items` balloon flying along the HUD, here from the lemming icon (its x read from `Hud_Draw`'s mov at 0x42A5E8, which Lomax+ moves for widescreen), to the lives, where `Item_UpdateCoinLifeToHud` adds the life (up to 30). With a coin's 1UP already on its way the life is added at once (the flag is shared). FLINK.DLL: `enemy_defeated`, `enemy_1up` (enemy.c). It lasts for the whole game, as Lomax's count does (cleared by `Game_ResetNewGame`). Lomax's bonus level can't be reached: `world_load` also clears `g_bBonusTrapdoorOpen` (0x59C718) in Flink's levels. FLINK.DLL: `enemy_defeated`, for every enemy kind.

**City dummy** (Flink's init 0x2FBD6, think 0x2FC30, walk reset 0x2FDBE, death 0x2FDE2; FLINK.DLL `city.c`, run by `dummy_update`). The Forest Dummy's walking, stun, stomps and attacks, pick-up, death and coin, and:
- **Standing.** It walks at 0.5 px a frame for 160 frames at first, then 100 + (random & 255) each time (0x2FDBE). When the count runs out its speed becomes 0; stopped, it stands (`STAY`, 8 frames at speed 25, played once) and then walks on with a new count.
- **Seeing Lomax** (0x2FD62): within 96 px across and 80 px up or down, on the side it faces, it charges (`ATCK`) at 1.5 px a frame (the walker speeds it up by 0x1000 a frame). Standing, on `STAY`'s frame 5 (its head turned) it looks behind it instead, and turns to Lomax if he is there.
- **Charging.** With Lomax behind it, it charges on for 30 frames and then walks; with him more than 128 px ahead it walks at once. Walls and ledges turn it as when walking. Its lance tip touching Lomax (0x1A41C, against his hitbox, not while he is hurt) hurts him as Flink's `Player_TakeHit` (0x1A7DC); it then turns round and walks. Its body hurts as any enemy's.
- **Stunned** it walks, not charges, after (0x2FC84), and its `STUN` frames have no lance.
- **Its death.** Stomped while stunned, attacked or hit by a thrown object it dies as the Forest Dummy, and its lance flies off (`city_lance`): Flink copies the actor (0x32CD8), sets `LANS` and throws it 3.0 px up and 1.5 px a frame the way the dummy faced, falling as a dead enemy (0x1B12E); the copy's +3 is cleared, so the lance is drawn unmirrored. A carried dummy thrown into something breaks through Flink's `Enemy_Die` (0x325DE), with no lance (`enemy_grab_break` clears `city` first).

**Mountain dummy** (Flink's init 0x2FE30, think 0x2FE96, walk reset 0x2FFD6, death 0x2FFF2; FLINK.DLL `mountain.c`, run by `dummy_update`). The Forest Dummy's walking, stun, stomps and attacks, pick-up, death and coin, and:
- **Standing.** It walks at 0.5 px a frame for 160 frames at first, then 100 + (random & 255) each time (0x2FFD6; a stomp starts a new count too). When the count runs out its speed becomes 0 and, stopped, it stands for 184 frames (`STAY` at the animation speed 0x20 whatever the record's: a frame every 8, through its 18 frames, then frames 0–4 again), looking behind it on frames 9–16, then walks on.
- **Its shield** (0x2FEBC). Flink's thrown objects set the hit flag of what they touch, and the dummy's think looks at the hitter: Flink's Shot (think 0x32630, the light throw straight on: a small stone, a stunned dummy) coming at the shield, while the dummy walks or stands, is turned back (the shot's vx negated, mirrored), the dummy's vx set to 0 (it speeds up again), and for 12 frames (+0x4C) further shots touching it are ignored. The shield faces the way it walks, and behind it from the 80th to the 143rd frame standing ((184 − frames left) >> 3 in 10…17: its look back). A shot from behind, a heavy thrown object (big stone, chest: Actor_ThrownThink 0x323C2), or any hit while it is stunned kills it. Here grab.c's `strike` asks `moun_shield` before `enemy_struck`; a shot the shield took gets its puff, as for any touch. Lomax's attacks, which Flink doesn't have (spin, roll down a slope, the thrown helmet), are blocked too: with Lomax on the side the shield faces (his x against the dummy's, the look back included) they only clank (Lomax's ROCKHITX, as on the Statue; `moun_blocks`, from `dummy_update`). From behind, or while it is stunned, they work as on the Forest Dummy (the first stuns, the roll kills). Lomax hurts it only by stomping on it or from behind.
- **Its death** (0x2FFF2): the shield flies off as the City dummy's lance (`flying_piece` in `city.c`: a copy of the actor, 0x32CD8, with `SHLD`, not mirrored, thrown 3.0 px up and 1.5 px a frame the way it faced). A thrown dummy that breaks dies by Enemy_Die (0x325DE) and drops none.

**Intree dummy** (Flink's init 0x2FA20, think 0x2FA96; FLINK.DLL `intree.c`, run by `dummy_update`). The Forest Dummy's stun, stomps and attacks, pick-up, death and coin, and:
- **Walking.** Flink's walker (0x1D0C4) with the Forest Dummy's flags 0x1000 at 0.5 px a frame, at full speed from the start. A ledge ahead sets the walker's stopping-to-turn bit, which its think takes as a jump: it hops off ledges instead of turning back. Down a 45° slope it slides (`SL45`); at one going up it turns.
- **Jumping** (0x2FB30): 3.0 px a frame up (`JUMP`, played once and held on its last frame), at a ledge and every 128 + (random & 127) frames on the ground (+0x4C); about 32 px high. In the air the walker's gravity is 0x2400 a frame and its speed goes up by 0x800 a frame toward 2.0. Flink's sound 0x39 (PCM 33) on the screen; here Lomax's `BOUNCE1` (0).
- **Gliding** (0x2FADE): falling, it flies (`FLY `) and its fall is cut to 0.5 px a frame after the walker's gravity (it moves about 0.64 px a frame down), its speed back down by 0x1000 a frame to 0.5. While it glides the walker runs with bit 15: no `JUMP` where the ground ends, and a 45° slope going down turns it instead of sliding.
- **Small** (+6 bit 1): carried 6 px forward and 6 down (+0x10 / +0x12), thrown as Flink's small shot (`SHSM`), as the Little Dude. One that falls out of the map is removed for good, with no coin, as the Ingredient Holder.

**Worm** (Flink's init 0x28A1C, think 0x28AE2, draw 0x28C32; FLINK.DLL `worm.c`, its own update and draw).
- **Its segments.** Four (+0x48: x, y and z words), 16 px apart on the placement's line, the head (segment 0) in front: facing left at x − 16, x, x + 16, x + 32. Each segment's y carries its own direction (negative: going left). The actor sits between segments 1 and 2 (x their middle, y the middle of their y) with the `DEAD` record, whose hitbox (−25, −21 … 24, −1) covers the worm.
- **Inching along.** A 64-frame timer (+0x46, from 0x40 down) moves it while its bit 4 is set (63…48 and 31…16), by the tables at 0x28BDE (bit 5 set) and 0x28BFE, their second halves on odd frames: per segment 1 px in x or none, and −1, 0 or 1 px in z. The rear and middle catch up while the head and the third segment rise 8 px, then the head reaches forward and they come down. Every segment goes 16 px each 64 frames: 0.25 px a frame.
- **Ground and turning.** A segment that moves looks for ground at its new x (`Map_GetGroundHeight` 0x1AEEA: 4 cells down from 16 px above its y; Lomax's `Plane_FindGroundY` with 4 cells, none off the map's sides). Within 4 px of its y it follows it (up and down gentle slopes), otherwise (a ledge, a wall, a step) that segment alone turns back. The head turns first and the others follow it one by one, so the worm folds and goes back with its head in front again.
- **Drawn** (0x28C32): `HEAD`'s frame (timer bit 5) at segment 0 and `BODY`'s at the others, flipped left-right going left, the head in front. Each is its frame's one hardware sprite (0x34132, drawn by `Sprite_DrawScreen` 0x3430E without its offset inside the ANIM frame), its top-left at (x − 12, y + z − 32), the body's at y + z − 24: 24 px wide, the head 32 tall and the body 24, both resting on the segment's y. Lomax's sprites are the whole ANIM frame (24 × 32), in which the body's sprite is 8 px down, so FLINK.DLL draws the body's frame at y + z − 32 (`WORM_BODY_DY`; the converter's `worm_records` checks those offsets, 0 for `HEAD`, 8 for `BODY`).
- **Hits.** Touching it hurts. A stomp (Actor_CheckStompedByPlayer 0x1A352) bounces Lomax off it with sound 0x18 (`SND_HIT`) and nothing else: it can't be stomped dead. A thrown object kills it wherever it hits (Flink's hit flag, +3 bit 6, 0x28C1E). Lomax's spin, roll and thrown helmet, which Flink doesn't have, kill it when they hit its head (the head sprite's box against Lomax's hitbox, or the thrown helmet's: the actor run by `ThrownHelmet_Update` 0x42F660) elsewhere the spin and roll hurt Lomax (`Player_TakeHit`) and the helmet bounces off, flying back to him as after any hit.
- **Its death** (0x28C1E, then `Actor_ForgetForever` 0x1B1E8 and the break-up 0x1B1F8): mirrored if its head goes left, each of the four sprites of its `DEAD` frame (the head and three body pieces in a line) flies off on its own from its place in the frame (mirrored: x − dx − 24, the ANIM's width) at vx ((random & 31) − 15) / 8 and 1.0 + (random & 31) / 8 px a frame up, falling 0x4000 a frame (0x1B388), until none is on the screen (here, or 300 ticks). Sound 0x2A (here `SND_HIT`, as the other enemies' deaths), the coin and the defeated count, and it stays dead for the level visit.
- Flink's draw hook 0x28C82 removes a Worm that a Generator spawns while the screen shows it (never drawn, gone after 30 frames, 0x328BA); here it appears, as every Generator's object (see Hidden objects).

**Yeti** (Flink's `Yeti`: init 0x30044, think 0x30096, its dust 0x30382; FLINK.DLL `yeti.c`, its own update and draw).
- **Asleep and waking.** It sleeps (`STAY`) until Lomax is within 32 px up or down and 128 px across (0x30102), then turns to him and wakes (`WARN`) for 20 frames.
- **Spinning** (0x301DE). For 180 + (random & 255) frames it spins (`SPIN`, with Flink's sound 0x1A at the start) along the ground as Flink's Actor_Walk with flags 0xD000 (a wall turns it, a 45° slope turns it either way, it runs off ledges and falls), from 0.5 px a frame, faster by 0x200 a frame up to 1.75. With Lomax behind it for 40 frames it stops and turns to him (back to 0.5). Its feet throw out clods, one every 16 frames (2.5 px up, a random 0–1.9 px across; 4 at most, each flying 64 frames). Then its speed is 0; stopped on the ground it is **tired** (`WAIT`, facing right) for 60 + (random & 127) frames, and then asleep, or waking again if Lomax is near.
- **Hits.** Touching it always hurts. Not spinning (asleep, waking, tired) a stomp, any of Lomax's attacks or a thrown object kills it (Flink: a stomp, a hit). Spinning it can't be stomped (landing on it hurts: `yeti_spiked`, contact.c), and Lomax's spin and roll do nothing to it; only his thrown helmet reaches it and leaves it tired, as when its spin runs out (in Flink only his slide and spells kill it then). A thrown object hitting it while it spins is turned back (0x302B8: the object's vx negated, mirrored, the Yeti's vx 0, sound 0x29: Lomax's HELMHIT1; `yeti_deflects` from grab.c's `strike`), and for 14 frames hits don't count.
- **It hits other enemies.** Spinning, it hits the first enemy its box touches, as a thrown object does (Actor_FindTouchingActor 0x1A5A0): a Mountain dummy or a sleeping Yeti dies, a Yeti turns it back.

**Meta dummy** (Flink's init 0x23618, think 0x2365A; FLINK.DLL `metadummy.c`, whose header has every number). A flying head on Flink's draw list 1. The Forest Dummy's stun, stomps and attacks, pick-up, death and coin, and:
- **Idle.** It hovers where it was placed and bobs 4 px up and down every 32 frames (a random phase each), its speed braked by 0x400 a frame.
- **Chasing.** Once Lomax is within 100 px across and 80 px up or down of its y + 20, it flies after him: Actor_MoveToward (0x1B008) toward his x and his feet − 20, 0x400 a frame (twice that while still moving away), up to 1.0 px a frame on each axis, facing him. It keeps on while he is within 120 px across and 100 px up or down, then brakes and bobs where it is. Touching it hurts.
- **Stomped** (sound 0x48, an FM sound: Lomax's enemy hit): stunned for 300 frames, it pops up and falls to the ground (`STUN`, Flink's shared stun 0x1D074), harmless and carryable; then `FLY ` again, rising at 1.0 braked by 0x400 a frame (32 px), and back to its idle bob. Stomped while stunned, or hit by a thrown object or Lomax's roll, it dies (`DEAD`, Enemy_Die).

**Storm Cloud** (Flink's init 0x231B2, think 0x23266, draw 0x2346E; FLINK.DLL `storm.c`, whose header has every number). A dark cloud on the ground, on Flink's draw list 2 (behind Lomax). A map's first Storm Cloud (spawn flag bit 6) only builds the shrunk frames in Flink and is left out.
- **Moving.** It drifts left at 0.5 px a frame along the ground, 10 px over it, bobbing 0–4 px every 64 frames (Actor_SnapToGround 0x1AE96: where the ground ends or steps more than 3–4 px it turns back). It **breathes**: every 64 frames it shrinks to 85% and back, in 5% steps about the middle of its frame (Flink's four prebuilt scaled frames; here `Actor_Draw` at that scale). Its mouth (`WOLK`'s piece with selector 1) is hidden.
- **Blowing.** 60 frames after it starts, and then 80–207 frames after each blow, once its breath is full, it stops for 90 frames, opens its mouth and blows (sound 0x3A on the screen): every 4th frame a puff (`WIND`, 9 at most) flies out of its mouth, 22 px in front of it, at 2.5–3.4 px a frame, rising faster each frame, for 28–35 frames. Lomax in front of it is pushed away by (180 − (|dx| + 2 |dy|)) × 0x444 a frame (0.75 px a frame at the mouth, nothing 180 px off; dx, dy from the mouth to his feet − 20), on the ground or in the air. Here that push is scaled as Lomax's walk is (1.5 × Flink's, then `move_speed`), added before `Player_Update` (`storm_before`), and never into a wall in front of him.
- **Hits.** Touching it hurts. A stomp, any of Lomax's attacks or a thrown object kills it: its puffs vanish and its `DEAD` frame (a burst of puffs) breaks up into its sprites (Actor_ForgetForever 0x1B1E8, sound 0x2A; here `Actor_ShatterIntoPieces` with `BOOM`), gone for good, a spirit and maybe a coin.

**Blitz dude** (Flink's init 0x22DA2, think 0x22E60, the lightning's think 0x23004; FLINK.DLL `blitz.c`, whose header has every number). A man sitting on a cloud, on Flink's draw list 2 (behind Lomax), the cloud behind him (draw list 3).
- **Following.** Once his think runs he is never culled (Flink's activation window 0x18). He flies toward a point over Lomax: his x + a random −32..31 px, his feet − 120 + a random −16..15 px, by Actor_MoveToward (0x1B008) at his own random acceleration (0x600..0x7F0 a frame, twice that while moving away) up to his own random top speed (1.5..1.94 px a frame). A count starts at 60 frames; it runs down only while Lomax is on the ground (not in the air, not on a platform: then it is held at 5 or more).
- **Striking.** When the count runs out, Lomax's place then is the target and the cloud warns (`WARN`). He flies over it (0x2000 a frame, up to 1.5) and, from 60 frames on, once he is nearly still (|vx| + |vy| under 0.25) within 8 px of it, the lightning (`ZAP `, half a frame a tick, 70 ticks) strikes the target from his cloud down. Its 6th frame on the screen shakes the screen 0x23 frames with sound 0x2D (when no shake runs); from its 9th to its 14th frame its box, 50 × 25 px on the ground, hurts Lomax and hits the first actor it touches as a thrown object does. 60 frames after the strike he follows again for 80–143 frames. Flink also flashes the screen; Lomax has no counterpart.
- **Hits.** Touching him hurts. A stomp, any of Lomax's attacks or a thrown object kills him (Enemy_Die: `DEAD`, falling, gone for good, a spirit and maybe a coin).
- **The cloud let go** (0x230AA, then the Wolky platform's think 0x23120). When he dies his cloud stays where it was and rises at 0.25 px a frame, swaying 5 px each way every 128 frames. It is a platform (`Platform_MoveAndCarryPlayer`): landed on, it sinks at 0.25 for 20 frames, then rises again with Lomax on it. It goes at y −30 (off the top of the map) or 128 px off the view.
- **Here** the cloud and the lightning are `Actor_Alloc` children of his type: the cloud drawn at his point + (0, 8) (`STAY`'s attach point, Flink's hook 0x230C2), hidden with him while he waits for Flink's window, gone with him if he is despawned.

**Circling suckers** (Flink's init 0x237D8, think 0x238A6, draw 0x23944; FLINK.DLL `circ.c`, whose header has every number). A ring of heads around the placement's point (META's `FLY ` heads), on Flink's draw list 1. In 30-1 each ring is spawned by a Generator at its x, so it appears around Lomax as he walks through.
- **The ring.** The placement's actor makes `how many` heads (16 in every converted ring), evenly spaced round the circle, and goes. Each head turns round the point by `turn-speed` (1024 a turn: 3–6, a turn in 3.4–5.7 s) at the `radius` (200 px), which shrinks by `shrink-speed` / 400 px a frame (150 or 175: 0.37–0.43 px). Each head flaps between `FLY `'s two frames at its own random speed.
- **Closing in.** Under 50 px the heads blink (hidden every other frame, still touching); under 20 px they are gone. A head off the screen and outside Flink's mid window (128 px left and right, 112 above, 144 below) is gone too.
- **Hits.** Touching a head hurts, blinking or not. A stomp, any of Lomax's attacks or a thrown object kills it on its own (Enemy_Die: `DEAD`, falling, a spirit and maybe a coin); the others go on.
- **Once a life.** The placement's actor is freed (Flink's Actor_Free, here `Actor_Kill`), so its placement stays spawned: the Generator doesn't make the ring again until a life is lost. The heads have no placement of their own (Flink's spare pending bit), so a killed head isn't remembered.
- **Here** the heads are `Actor_Alloc` children, never culled by Lomax; the mid window is taken around Lomax's view, as wide as Lomax+ makes it. Flink draws a head's hardware sprite 1 px right of where `FLY `'s pieces put it, so the converter writes `FLY ` at the draw's offsets (`circ_records`).

**Buzzard** (Flink's `Buzzard`: init 0x27740, think 0x2777E; FLINK.DLL `buzzard.c`, whose header has every number). On Flink's draw list 2 (behind Lomax).
- **Perched** (`STAY`). It waits on its tree until, with it on the screen, Lomax is at least 40 px below it, within `distance` px across, on the side it faces, and **facing away from it** (Flink's 0x278C2 tests his facing flag against the side he is on: it strikes from behind). Then it waits `wait-timer` more frames and takes off (sound 0x43: Lomax's ZOOM1, Lomax has no Buzzard).
- **The arc** (0x277A4, `FLY `, its frame set by the think, not animated). Its speed across goes by 1/16 a frame to 2.0 the way it faces; diving (frame 0) it falls faster by 1/8 a frame up to 3.0, and once the ground (searched 7 cells down from 16 px above it, Map_GetGroundHeight 0x1AEEA) is 30 px or less under it, it glides (frame 1): with ground under it, it keeps to 30 px over it (vy ± 1/8 a frame, within ±1.0, so it overshoots the dive and swings back up); with none (a gap) it climbs, 1/16 a frame faster with no limit, and flies up off the screen. Off the screen it is freed (Actor_Free 0x32D9E: no despawn, so it comes back only after a lost life; here Actor_Kill, its placement left spawned). A `wait-timer` of 0 flies from the start, and since Flink spawns it off the screen its first frame frees it: 19-1's Buzzard at 545,136 is never seen, in Flink or here.
- **Hits.** Touching it hurts, perched or flying. A stomp, any of Lomax's attacks (spin, roll, thrown helmet: as Flink's slide and spells) or a thrown object kills it: its `DEAD` frame breaks up into its sprites (Actor_ForgetForever 0x1B1E8; here `Actor_ShatterIntoPieces` with `BOOM`), gone for good, a spirit and maybe a coin (sound 0x48: Lomax's enemy hit).
- "On the screen" is Flink's +6 bit 4, set when the actor drew a sprite the frame before; here the Buzzard within Lomax's view (32 px margin, `enemy_on_screen`), taken after each update, a new Buzzard not seen yet.

**Razorbacks** (Flink's `Razorback`: init 0x27486, the spawner's think 0x274BE, the Razorbacks' 0x275BA; FLINK.DLL `razorback.c`, whose header has every number). The hint at 21-1's start: "A stampede of razorbacks heading this way! Quick, seek higher ground!"
- **The spawner.** The placement draws nothing and touches nothing. Once Flink's window reaches it (as any enemy: `enemy_wait`), it sends a Razorback 10 frames later and then every 30 frames, for `time` frames; then it waits until its Razorbacks are gone and frees itself (not spawned again before a lost life). A Razorback starts at the spawner's y, 100 px off the screen: left of it running right, or right of it running left with `move left` (Flink: the camera's x - 100 or + 420; here right of Lomax's wider view, at the map's last column at most), at (20 + rand & 15) / 8 px a frame (2.5 to 4.375), grunting every 50 + (rand & 15) frames on the screen (sound 0x18: Lomax's ROCKHITX, as the Statue's).
- **Not killable by Lomax** (not Flink's: the level is about avoiding them): in Flink his slide (0x1A462), spells and thrown objects kill a Razorback; here his spin, roll and helmet do nothing to it and thrown objects go through it (`enemy_struck` leaves it alone). No Razorback counts as defeated (no spirit, coin or HUD count).
- **The run.** Each frame it moves by its speed and keeps to the ground under it (Actor_SnapToGround 0x1AE96, 3 cells down, within 0x1AECA's reach at its speed). Touching it hurts Lomax; landing on it hurts too (Flink's Razorback has no stomp: `razorback_spiked`, contact.c). It is on Flink's draw list 2 (behind Lomax).
- **How they die.** Enemy_Die (0x1B12E): `DEAD`, thrown up at 4.0 with its running speed, falling through everything, freed off the screen (sound 0x22: Lomax's enemy hit). A Razorback dies:
  - where the ground under it ends: a pit, a ledge, the map's end (21-1's pits at x 1030 and 1730);
  - running into a rock (Flink's `Rock`: see Rocks): Actor_FindTouchingActor (0x1A5A0) finds the first actor it touches with +6 bit 3, in slot order, testing the even slots on even frames and the odd ones on odd frames; a rock found kills it, and the rock counts the hit and turns the dead Razorback back (`rock_hit`);
  - after another Razorback touched it (0x1A6D2 sets its hit flag, the toucher in +0x34): a running Razorback's hit is let be, but if the toucher has died by the time this Razorback's think looks (it ran off an edge or into a rock after the touch), this Razorback dies of it. So Razorbacks bunched behind a dying one die with it;
  - another Flink enemy it touches is hit as by a thrown object (none in 21-1).
- **The shake.** Flink's camera shake counter (0x874E) is set by each running spawner: with n Razorbacks alive, when the counter & 3 is 0, d = min(n + 1, 8) / 2 and the counter is set to d x 4 if that is more than the counter / 8; Camera_Update (0x19F84) takes 1 off a frame and adds the word at 0x19FB4 + (counter & ~1) to the camera's y (0, 0, -1, 1, -2, 2, -3, 3, -2, 2, -3, 3, -4, 4, -3, 3, -4, 4). So the screen shakes as long as a spawner runs, from -1 px every 4 frames with no Razorback to -2, +3, +3, -3 px with 7 or more. Here `g_quake` is that counter, its offset added to the camera's y after Player_Update (`razorback_after`) and taken off before the next one (`razorback_before`); Lomax's own `g_ScreenShake` isn't used.

**Cyclin' Dude, Chopper Bike, Little Dude** (Flink's inits 0x3045A, 0x30724, 0x3099C; FLINK.DLL `cyclindude.c`, whose header comment has every number):
- **The Cyclin' Dude** spawns his bike and hangs from it, flying toward his own spot over Lomax (x + (random & 63) − 32, y − 100 + (random & 31) − 16; `Actor_MoveToward` 0x1B008: 0x800 a frame, twice that while moving away, up to 2.0). Touching him hurts; flying, he can't be stomped. His `HANG` hooks put the bike at hook 1 and drop a Little Dude from hook 2 every 240 frames, the first at once: 2.0 up and 0.5–2.4 px across, left and right in turn.
- **Hit by a thrown object** (0x30508): a small one (a Little Dude, a small stone: Flink's +6 bit 1) knocks him off. The bike is let go and he falls (3.0 up, `DEAD` in the air), then walks as the Forest Dummy at 1.0 px a frame; 30 frames after Lomax gets behind him he turns to him if Lomax is still there. For 30 frames after the fall hits don't count; then a hit or a thrown object kills him, and a stomp does at any time. A big object (a dummy, a big stone) kills him and his bike (0x30540). Lomax's attacks (spin, tumble, helmet: Flink's spells) knock him off, as a small object does.
- **The bike, let go**, falls at 1.5 px slowing by 0x800 a frame, then rises at 0.25 (0x30754). Flink takes it with the grab button as any light object; Lomax takes hold of it just by touching it, as of his ropes (as for the lianas; not for 30 ticks after jumping off it), and **rides it** (0x307B2): he hangs from it in his rope pose, the bike at Flink's hand (45 px over his feet). Left / Right speed him up by 0x1000 a frame to 1.0 (plus Flink's air control, 0.125 a frame); Up / Down by 0x2000 to 1.0 over or under the bike's lift, which starts at 0.25 up and drops by 0x100 a frame of riding: the bike gets heavier, and after about 5 s Up only slows the fall. He stays below y 30 and 20 px inside the map's sides; walls and ceilings stop him. Jump (pressed after it was released) jumps off at half a jump's speed; the bike flies on and can be taken again. Being hurt or landing ends the ride and the bike breaks (Enemy_Die).
- **The Little Dude** falls at 0.125 a frame as `DEAD` and lands walking toward Lomax (`WALK`; Flink's walker with flags 0xD000 at 1.0: it turns at walls and on 45° slopes and walks off ledges, so one that lands in the middle of a 45° slope turns there every frame, as in Flink). A stomp stuns it for 300 frames as the Forest Dummy: it can then be picked up crouching (light, small, its stun the fuse, thrown as the small shot), and it hops back up when the stun ends. Stomped again or hit, it dies. Touching it hurts.

**Firemonster** (Flink's think 0x2F2D8, the mouth's hook 0x2F376 and the flame 0x2F3B6):
- **Walking.** Flink's walker with flags 0x9800 at 1/3 px a frame: as the dummy it turns at walls and at ledges, but it turns on a 45° slope either way, and where the ground under it ends it falls (gravity 0x2400 a frame, while its speed goes to 2.0 px) instead of turning back. A timer of 120 + (random & 127) frames runs down; then it stops on `WALK`'s second frame, mouth open.
- **Its face.** `WALK` holds two heads: the open-mouthed attack face is an extra piece with hide selector 1 (piece flags 0x61). Flink's `Actor_DrawFrame` (0x328C8) skips a piece with selector n while bit 7 + n of the actor's state word is set; the Firemonster sets bit 8 when it spawns and while it walks, and clears it while it breathes (0x2F240, 0x2F340, 0x2F2F4). The converter keeps the selector as the piece's Lomax slot for enemies, and FLINK.DLL sets Lomax's hide mask (actor +0x70, bit 1) while it walks, so the attack face shows only during the breath. `BOOM` leaves it out.
- **Breathing** (160 frames, counted on that frame): a smoke puff rises from the mouth for the first 32 and the last 64 frames (`DUST`, mirrored when it faces left). From 32 to 95 the flame: up to 5 segments of `FIRE`, each 8 px further forward and 8 px lower than the last, but never less than 16 px over the ground below, wobbling a few px each frame. Late in it the segments nearest the mouth go out first, so the flame leaves the mouth, and once it is 5 long a `FADE` tip rises at its end. At 30 the breath sound (Flink's 0x32; Lomax has no fire sound, so ZOOM1, 60). Then it walks again with a new timer.
- **The flame hurts**: each frame Flink tests one point past a segment against Flink's box (cycling through the segments). Here the same point against Lomax's hitbox, then `Player_TakeHit`. Flink's test also runs for segments that have gone out, so its invisible flame hurts; here only drawn segments do. Touching the monster hurts too.
- **One stomp**, or Lomax's attacks, destroys it (Flink's 0x1B1E8, also for its slide and spells). Flink breaks its current frame up into its sprites, which fly up and fall; here Lomax's `Actor_ShatterIntoPieces` (0x430690) does that with `BOOM`. It is gone for good, counts as defeated and drops a coin as the dummy does.
- **Drawing.** FLINK.DLL draws the flames and smoke with `Sprite_QueueWorld` (0x40AA70), one draw bucket over the monster, from its draw function (`fire_draw`).

**Ingredient Holder** (Flink's think 0x2F5CC; FLINK.DLL `holder.c`). In Flink it carries an ingredient in its bag and drops it on its second hit. Lomax has no ingredients, so its bag is empty and it drops a coin as the other enemies.
- **Walking.** Flink's walker (0x1D0C4) with flags 0x800 at 0.5 px a frame. Unlike the dummy it jumps: at a ledge it looks for ground 16, 32, 48 and 64 px ahead (from 48 px above its feet down 7 cells) and jumps there (`JUMP`, up (height difference) / (distance) + distance / 16 px a frame; then gravity 0x2400 a frame while its speed goes to 2.0 px). With no ground that close it stops and turns. It jumps up a 45° slope (4.0 px up) and slides down one (`SL45`). Its `WALK` steps at its speed / 1000 (Flink's anim speed, 0x2F5F2). Touching it hurts.
- **First hit** (a stomp, or one of Lomax's attacks, counted as the dummy counts them; 0x2F628): it swaps its head and arm for scared ones. Its `WALK` pieces carry hide selectors: the calm ones 1 and 4, the scared ones 2 and 3; Flink's state word hides 2 and 3 from its init (0x8600) and 1 and 4 after the hit. FLINK.DLL sets Lomax's hide mask (+0x70) the same way. For 60 frames it blinks (hidden every 4th frame) and nothing touches it; it runs at 1.125 px a frame for good, facing away from where Lomax faces, and without flag 0x800, so at a ledge with no ground close it jumps off (2.0 px up).
- **Second hit** (stomp or attack; in Flink its slide and spells kill it at once): it dies as the dummy does (`DEAD`, thrown up, falls through the map), counts as defeated and drops a coin. Flink drops the bag's ingredient there (`Item_Spawn` 0x2C21A).
- **Out of the map.** One that falls out of the map is removed for good, with no coin (in Flink, Actor_Walk flags it and it dies). The map's left and right edges are walls for it here: scared, it jumped off 1-2's left edge, where Lomax can't follow.
- Not converted: the ceiling check of a jump (Flink's 0x1ADDE, 2.0 px down when its head meets a solid cell).

**Thief** (Flink's think 0x2F718; FLINK.DLL `thief.c`). Close to Lomax's own coin thief (object 44), but slower; in Flink it steals one of the ingredients Flink carries and runs off with it. Here it steals Lomax's coins.
- **Waiting.** It stands (`STAY`) until Lomax is within 96 px and 32 px up or down. Then it turns to him, gasps (`GASP`, 20 frames) and chases him at 1.5 px a frame with the holder's walker (flag 0x800: it jumps gaps and turns at ledges; `jump_walk`). Facing away from him it goes on for 30 frames and stops; more than 128 px from him it stops at once. Stopped, it waits again.
- **Stealing.** Touching Lomax while it chases (Flink's `Actor_OverlapsPlayer` 0x1A41C, here Lomax's own contact: `enemy_touched` in contact.c, where the touch would hurt him) it takes his coins, 10 or all he has if fewer, and bumps him (Flink's 0x1A71A: 2.0 px the way it faces and 4.0 px up, no harm; sound: Lomax's thief's jump, 0x1D). Flink takes one ingredient, a random one of those he carries (`Thief_PickIngredient` 0x2F8AC). It turns and runs away for good at 1.25 px a frame, jumping off ledges (walker flag 0). It holds a Lomax coin in its hand (Flink draws the stolen ingredient at `WALK`'s attach point with flags 1): an `Items` coin from `Item_SpawnCoin` that FLINK.DLL keeps at the hand and that can't be picked up. Its arms swap through the hide selectors: from its init (state word 0x8800) the arm with the loot (selector 4) is hidden, after the steal (0x2F7F2) the empty arm (3). It doesn't hurt for 20 frames after a steal.
- **With no coins**, touching it hurts, as in Flink with no ingredients. Touching it while it waits, gasps or runs away hurts too.
- **One stomp**, or Lomax's attacks, kills it as the dummy dies, gone for good and counted as defeated, with the usual coin. If it holds coins it throws them all out, as Lomax's thief throws the coins it takes (the {vx, vy} table at 0x4645E8, vy − 0.5 px), and Lomax can pick them up again. Flink drops the stolen ingredient (`Item_Spawn`).
- **Getting away.** One that falls out of the map (in 2-1, into the water right of the first one) is gone for good with its coins. One that scrolls far enough off screen is despawned as any enemy, and its coins are lost; it comes back as a new thief.

**Fish** (Flink's `Visje`, think 0x2814C; FLINK.DLL `visje.c`). It lives in the water and jumps out of it on a timer.
- **Swimming.** With an "area" it swims back and forth around its placement, x = x0 + sin(phase) × area / 2, the phase stepping 4 of 1024 a frame (a turn in about 4 s), starting to the right and turning at each end. With none it swims straight on at "speed" / 100 px a frame (left with "move left"), and Lomax despawns it off screen as any enemy. Meanwhile it bobs about 4 px up and down (y += sin / 65536 px a frame, the phase stepping 10). `SWIM`.
- **Jumping.** 30 frames after it appears, and then every "jump-timer" frames, it jumps at 6.0 px a frame up (about 95 px high), gravity 0.1875 px a frame. Falling into the water faster than 1.0 px a frame it is slowed to 1.0; back at its placement's height it swims again. It splashes (Lomax's `Water_SpawnSplash` 0x4320E0, sound 0x2C) as it crosses the surface either way: Flink's surface `g_WaterSurfaceY` (0x8718) is Lomax's `g_WaterLevelY` (0x59F1C4), the Water actor's y, the tide included. `JUMP` in the air.
- **Touching it** hurts Lomax. One stomp or attack kills it as the dummy dies, counted as defeated, with the usual coin. A "deadly" one (Flink clears its stompable bit, +6 bit 3) can't be killed: it isn't hittable here, so a stomp hurts Lomax and his attacks pass through it.
- The sine is Lomax's table (`G_SINTABLE`), within 1% of Flink's (0x1491E).

**Statue** (Flink's init 0x2829E, think 0x28358, the mouth's hook 0x283F4 and the shot 0x28448; FLINK.DLL `statue.c`). A stone head that stands still and spits fireballs.
- **Rising.** One that a Generator spawns (all but 5-1's first two) starts 40 px under its placement, hidden behind the ground, throws 5 clods (the chests' `Fx_SpawnDebris`, debris sound) and rises 2 px a frame. Here the ground hides it through the clip line (+0x7C), as the chests.
- **Shooting.** `STAY`'s attach point with hook 1 is the mouth. Every "period" frames (param 0, 125-240 in the maps; the first 30 frames after a risen one appears) it spawns the shot there: `SHOT`, which waits 30 frames at the mouth, then flies straight on at "speed" / 100 px a frame (2.0 in every map), left with "move left", through walls (sound: Flink's 0x2F; ZAP1 here). Flink counts the period only while the statue is drawn; here while it is within 16 px of the view, and a shot is removed 64 px out of it. The shot is an `Actor_Alloc` child of the statue, flagged to hurt only: touching it hurts Lomax, and nothing hits it.
- **Touching the statue** doesn't hurt. A stomp only clanks (Flink's sound 0x18, the locked chest's; ROCKHITX here) and Lomax bounces off. Lomax's spin and helmet count as a stomp.
- **Breaking it.** Flink's slide, its spells and thrown objects break it (0x28392): the shot still at its mouth goes out and `DEAD` breaks up (0x1B1E8). Here Lomax's roll down a slope (`TUMB`) and thrown objects (`enemy_struck`), with `Actor_ShatterIntoPieces` on `BOOM`. It is gone for good, counts as defeated and drops a coin as the other enemies.

**Ground Raketen wurm** (Flink's `Raketen wurm`: init 0x25AA6, think 0x25B94 / 0x25D36, draw 0x26016, death 0x26246; FLINK.DLL `wurm.c`). A spiky Raketen wurm that waits in a patch of mud and arches out of it at Lomax.
- **Waiting.** Its mud (`MUD `, flipped every 8 frames) lies at its placement. It faces Lomax, and when his x is 60 to 120 px from its own (at any height) it comes out. After it has been back in its mud for 120 frames it can come out again.
- **Coming out.** Its path position grows 8 a frame up to "length" × 8 (640–1248), then shrinks back below 0. The path runs from the sprite's top-left at (x + 48, y + 16): a quarter circle of radius 64 (Flink's sine table 0x1491E, 512 units), with the sprites' frames 2, 1 and 0 as it turns from rising to level, then level, 67 px up, (s − 512) / 5 px on, wiggling up to 4 px. Facing left it is mirrored around x − 68, hanging ("head-over-heals") around y − 32 and drawn upside down. There are position / 100 segments (Flink's `dbra` loop, none below 100): the head (`HEAD`) at the path position − 100 and a body segment (`TAIL`) every 100 units back to the mud (about 20 px apart). Draw order: the mud in front, then the segments, the head behind (Flink's sprite order). At position 240 on the way out it throws the chests' clods (hanging: only the debris sound).
- **Touching it** hurts: the head (the actor, `HEAD`'s hitbox, flagged hurts + hittable while out) through Lomax's contact loop, and every body segment in the sprite's middle 16 × 16 (Flink checks one segment in four a frame; here all, except while Lomax falls onto the head).
- **Stomping its head** bounces Lomax off it as off any enemy, and the Raketen wurm goes back into its mud at once; until it is in, nothing of it hurts or can be stomped (Flink: `Actor_CheckStompedByPlayer` on the head, sound 0x18, `g_PlayerBounceReq`). Lomax's spin, roll and thrown helmet on the head send it back too (`enemy_attacked`, with the hit sound 0x59).
- **Only a thrown object kills it** (in Flink also its slide and spells): touching the head or any segment, even while it goes back (`wurm_struck`, from grab.c's `strike`). Its head and segments fly apart as Flink's (each up to 1 px a frame sideways and 1–5 px up, falling 0.25 px a frame per frame) and the mud goes. It is gone for good, counts as defeated and may drop a coin, as the other enemies.
- **The vertical Raketen wurm** ("height" h not 0: 4-2, 8-1; Flink's think 0x25C74, draw 0x25DE8). It comes out when Lomax is 40 to 80 px away, at 2 a frame, and rises out of its hole in a column into a second hole h px above (hanging: down into one h px below), where it disappears and waits (the next time it comes out of its own hole again). Its "length" / 16 segments, the head among them, all with the rising sprite (frame 2), are 16 px apart at x − 12, wiggling 0–4 px with their y and flipped left-right on alternate halves of each 64 px; only those between the two holes are drawn and touch. Its own hole's mud shows until the Raketen wurm has left it, the far hole's once the Raketen wurm reaches it; clods at position 50. A stomp, spin, roll or helmet on the head sends it back down the way it came; a thrown object kills it, as the arching Raketen wurm.
- **Drawing.** While it waits nothing of it is drawn, its mud neither: Flink's waiting think ends in 0x3333E, which leaves the actor off the draw list, so the mud shows only while the Raketen wurm is out (the vertical Raketen wurm's holes too). Going back after a stomp or hit it blinks, not drawn every 4th frame, as the Ingredient Holder after its first hit (Flink's doesn't blink). In 8-1 the Scroll Point keeps the camera's y, so Flink's 224-px screen ends at the map's height − 8 (y 263), just above the mud of the holes on the floor (y 264–271). Lomax's 240-px view reaches that strip, so FLINK.DLL doesn't draw that mud.
- **The actor** sits at the head while the Raketen wurm is out (so Lomax culls it by the head) and at its mud while it waits, touching nothing then.

**Puge** (Flink's `Puge`, Dutch "spugen", to spit: init 0x24F40, think 0x24FD2, draw 0x25108, rocks 0x25138; FLINK.DLL `puge.c`). The tree and its face are the map's background cells; the actor is only the open mouth over the face, and the rocks it drops.
- **Shut and open.** The mouth is shut (nothing drawn: Flink's +3 bit 1, hidden) for 31 frames after it appears, then open (`PUGE`) for 151, then shut for 'time' + 1, and so on.
- **Spitting.** 30 frames after it opens it spits two groups of 5 rocks from the mouth (Flink's particle groups 0x2E0C8, tables 0x250A4 / 0x250D6: delays 0–25 frames, a few px of spread, 0.25–0.75 px a frame sideways and up to 1.5 up), MAIN.WLD's clods `ERDE` (frames 0 and 1 in turn). Flink's sound 0x31 (PCM 27); Lomax has no spitting sound, so the debris sound (0x3D).
- **The rocks** fall with gravity 0x2000 a frame (at most 4.0), their vx braked by 0x400 a frame. Each bounces once, at half its speed, on the line 8 px over the ground under the tree (searched 20 cells down from the placement), then falls on through the ground. Unlike a chest's clods they don't run out (0x25154 holds their count): they show until the mouth shuts. A rock's 4 × 4 box (inclusive) touching Lomax's hitbox hurts him (0x1A46E, `Player_TakeHit`). Flink tests one rock of each group a frame; here every rock every tick.
- **Nothing else touches it**: the tree doesn't hurt, can't be stomped or hit, and never dies (not counted as an enemy). Lomax culls it off screen as any enemy.
- The rocks move in the update, not the draw (Lomax+'s FPS Override may draw several times a tick), and are drawn with `Sprite_QueueWorld` one bucket over the mouth, as the Firemonster's flames.

**The Forest Boss** (8-2: Flink's `Boss` init 0x2948C → 0x294B0, think 0x295D0 / 0x29602, stilts 0x299B4 / 0x29A46, spirits 0x2EDAA; FLINK.DLL `forestboss.c`). A furry monster on two stone stilts with spiked tops.
- **Arrival.** It waits 320 px above its placement until Lomax is within 50 px of its x, then drops in at 4.0 px a frame and the boss music starts (Flink's music index 8).
- **Moves.** It walks at 1 px a frame, right first, turning 160 px from the map's ends (`WALK` forward walking left, backward walking right). Each time it passes its placement's x it squats (`JUMP` 0–32), jumps straight up at 9.0, falls 0x3000 a frame faster (at most 4.0) and lands with a screen shake (Flink: 35 frames; here Lomax's `g_ScreenShake` = 10, as his falling tree sets it, with the Werewolf's landing sound) and gets up (`JUMP` 32–0). Lomax's shake moves the camera's y and is then clamped to 16 … `nMaxScrollY` (0x41226B), which took it all off in 8-2, a map one screen tall; in Flink's maps `shake_after` (from `player_tick`) puts the offset back after `Player_Update` (the next one restores the camera first, `g_CameraYBeforeShake`). Beyond the map's top or bottom edge a strip of the backdrop colour shows, as many pixels as the shake (±4 at 10).
- **Hits.** 8 (Flink's 0x8280). A stomp on its head (its hitbox, the record's), a thrown object or Lomax's spin, roll or helmet (`enemy_attacked`) hits it: it stops, `OUCH` for 40 frames, then 60 more in which nothing of its body hurts or is hit, and a spirit flies out of it. Touching its head hurts otherwise. Its head is 126 px above the floor: Lomax reaches it jumping from the tree tops at the arena's ends or the lianas; a heavy stone's arc from the floor falls short.
- **Stilts (the tree trunks it holds).** The points come from the frame's `LG..` record. As Flink's `NODE` think (0x299F2), following its code with two exceptions:
  - **Edges.** Each trunk hurts only at its two edges, x ± 6 by point − 54 … − 30 (the spiked top) and point + 74 … + 98 (the lower end), inclusive (0x1A46E: the box against the player's hitbox, then `Player_TakeHit`, which a hurt Lomax ignores). Exception: the lower end hurts only while the trunk is raised (its point more than 4 px above where it stands, y − 52: lifted in its step, or the boss in the air); on the ground it doesn't.
  - **Solid.** Both trunks, raised or not, are Flink's pushed objects (0x1AA56 from 0x29A3E), not while Lomax is hurt (0x29A34) or dying: the box is the `NODE` hitbox at y + 40 (point x − 9 … + 8, y − 14 … + 115; the left trunk mirrored, x − 8 … + 9); the side is the trunk's x against Lomax's; the check holds when the point 16 px in front of him toward it is inside the box across, his feet are below its top and his feet − 32 not below its bottom, except on the ground walking away from it. Flink then pushes it (its `PUSH`; the `NODE` goes back to the boss's point next frame). Exception: Lomax goes no further in, with no push pose (`trunks_after`, from `player_tick` after `Player_Update`): back to where the point in front of him is at its edge, or where he was at the tick's start if already in, and his speed into it is taken off. A trunk coming onto him doesn't push him out.
  - **Running under.** A trunk swinging forward in its step is lifted 40–55 px, so its box and lower end pass over Lomax (hitbox y − 41 … − 1), as they would over Flink: Lomax walking at the boss passes under the stepping trunk; only a planted one stops him, and the lower end hits him while a trunk is lifting or coming down (lifted less than about 26 px).
  - **Thrown objects.** Any trunk, raised or not, throws back a thrown object (or shot) flying into it, as Flink's 0x299B4: mirrored, vx negated and moved 4 × the new vx, with Lomax's stomp sound, effect 2 as `Player_BounceOffEnemy` plays it (Flink's 0x29); grab.c's `strike` asks `boss_trunk_bounce` after the enemies, the boss's body included: Flink's boss spawns its two `NODE`s into the first free slots right after taking its own (0x32CD8), and `Actor_FindTouchingActor` (0x1A5A0) searches in slot order, so an object touching the head and a trunk hits the head; it finds the body even while it can't be hit (its guard), and then stops there (a puff, no trunk). A trunk's turn-back then puffs, and a Forest Dummy thrown into it dies as on any hit. No push pose (`Actor_CheckPlayerPush`). Not ported: Flink's slide (`SL45`) knocking into a trunk (0x1A496).
- **Drawing.** The boss is on draw layer 0 (bucket plane + 3), behind the stones and thrown objects (layer 1, as the other enemies), which went behind it on the same layer. Lomax (layer 6) and the spirits (2) are in front.
- **Spirits.** Each hit and every 32 frames of its defeat set a spirit free, and one more as it breaks up (`enemy_defeated`); they are collected into the magic glass (see Spirits).
- **Defeat.** Lomax can't be hurt while it dies: Flink sets its hurt timer to −2 every frame (0x29724), and so does `boss_untouchable` (forestboss.c): Lomax's `Player_TakeHit` (0x412E30) does nothing while `g_PlayerHurtTimer` isn't 0, and a negative one counts back up to 0 by itself. Flink doesn't blink then, so `boss_quiet_after` (after Player_Update) clears the invulnerability blink Lomax gets for a negative timer (his +0x78). At the 8th hit the screen shakes and keeps shaking (at least 8) through the 161 frames and again at the break-up (Flink shakes 35 frames), the music changes to Flink's index 21, a fanfare played once (`music_play_once`, README "Music"; Lomax loops every track), and for 161 frames it is hit (`OUCH`), a spirit flying out every 32 (Flink also flashes the screen; not here). Then its body breaks into its sprites (`Actor_ShatterIntoPieces` on `BOOM`) and `DEAD`'s frames 1–4 (its head, its body, its two stilts with the hands) fly off whole, as Flink's 0x29906 throws them. It counts as defeated (the HUD's count, a spirit). Flink then drops its crystal and plays its end-of-level sequence; here nothing plays: 100 ticks after the break-up the Finish appears in the middle of the arena with a poof and the trapdoor sound, and Lomax leaves through it with Up as from any level. It is never culled (it would never come back: 0x2000). A defeated boss stays gone for the level visit (gone.c); after a lost life the arena has its Finish from the start (`boss_gone`).

**The Village Boss** (16-2: Flink's `Boss` init 0x2948C → 0x29A9E for VILLAGE, think 0x29CCC, the men 0x29F80 / 0x2A13C, their chains' draw 0x2A36E / 0x2A3F8 / 0x2A478, the ceiling's stones 0x2A2A6 / 0x2A2F2; FLINK.DLL `villageboss.c`). A walking machine with three men in glass domes on top, each on a chain that shoots up to the ceiling.
- **The endless screen.** Its init makes the map wrap around (0x8726) and the camera scroll on past the map's edges (`Camera_Update` 0x19EAE), at the map's Scroll Point's 1 px a frame. Here Lomax's own wrap, the airship's (`scroll_wrap`, see Scrolling screens). The Village Boss starts at x −500, running in from behind.
- **Its walk.** Running (`RUN `) at 2 px a frame until, at a frame 0, it is more than 260 px into Flink's 320-px screen (here the view − 60: Lomax+'s view is wider); standing (`STAY`) 60 + (rand & 127) frames, drifting back as the screen goes on; then walking (`WALK`) at the screen's pace for 200 + (rand & 127) frames if it is more than 60 px into the screen, else running. Each stop shoots a chain up: half the time the middle one, else both side ones (if the left one is down).
- **The side chains** grow 2 px a frame. At 76 px they hit the ceiling: the screen shakes, and a big stone is taken out of it and held between the two hands (the left hand's top + (21, 8), the chains bending to it). Back at 16 px they wait; when Lomax comes within 25 px of the Village Boss's x they go down and let the stone fall on him. It hurts him as it falls (Flink's lifetime is out), until it first comes to rest; then he can pick it up. (The rule that thrown objects never hurt Lomax is about his own throws.)
- **The middle chain** grows to 72 px (a shake), comes back to 20, goes up to 72 again (a shake) and down. At the first top three big stones come out of the ceiling (at its x − 64 + d, d random, then twice d + 32 … 63 mod 128), sink to y 35 and fall at the next shake (either chain's) with a small random vx, at half the gravity and no faster than 3.75 above y 90. They hit nothing while their speed is odd (Flink's 0x3256A), so they hurt Lomax only once they reach the full 4.0, low down.
- **What comes in.** Every 100–163 / 300–427 frames, turn about, a big stone and a Jumppad come in 40 px past the screen's right edge on the ground (y 208): the ammunition, and the way up to the men. They go 160 px left of the screen (Flink's widest window), unless Lomax carries one. The view is the map's width − `nMaxScrollX` (512 at Lomax+'s widest view, where `nMaxScrollX` is 0).
- **The men** are drawn at the frames' points (hook table 0x2A534): each chain from under its man up by its length (eased below 32 px, then 1.5 px a px), a link every 6 px of length, its head first, then the man behind it, the Village Boss last (the first drawn is in front, in Lomax's buckets as in Flink's sprite list). A side man is drawn while he can be hit, the middle one while any is left. For Lomax+'s FPS Override, which moves what each `Actor_Draw` call queued by that call's point and pairs the calls of two ticks by actor and n-th call: every part is drawn on its own from its own point, by a stand-in actor (a copy of the Village Boss's struct outside the game's slots) kept for that part, one per body part (sprite bank, side, rank in the frame along the axis that kind's pieces spread on: a thigh is three copies of one sprite laid along it, ranked by x; a shin two, one above the other, by y; ranked top to bottom, the thighs' copies would change places, most at Flink's sprite flip in RUN frame 23, and slide across each other between ticks) and one per chain. The body's pieces come from `XRUN` / `XWAL` / `XSTA` (the converter: `RUN `, `WALK`, `STAY`'s pieces, each its own one-piece frame drawn around the middle of its drawn pixels, `Sprites.centre`), and each stand-in stands at that middle: a limb turns by a new frame each tick with another box, so its corner jumps (10-13 px where Flink flips the thigh sprite, `RUN ` frames 22-23 and 31-32) while its middle moves as the limb does (at most 2 px there); from the corner, Lomax+ would draw the flipped thigh that far off between ticks and the shins would shake. Drawn whole, the legs, which move on their own, would shake running (a planted foot sliding with the body and jumping back at the tick), and the three chains, drawn by one actor in the frame's order of the men, would be paired with each other when that order changed. Neither the Village Boss nor the men hurt Lomax, and they can't be stomped.
- **Hits.** A thrown object hitting a man counts, a shot too (grab.c's `strike` asks `vboss_struck` first; Flink's 0x29F8E lets a shot fly on): it bounces up at 3.0 (odd: no more hits). Not Flink's: Lomax's stomp on a man (Flink's stomp test on the man's box, `flink_stomp_box`, with his bounce) and his spin, roll or thrown helmet reaching one (`player_attack_box`) count the same, 30 ticks apart (`lomax_hits`); Lomax reaches the men from a Jumppad. Either side man falls dead (`MEN5`) with a spirit. The middle one can be hit only once both are gone: the first hit only hurts him (`MEN2`); the second (not while Lomax dies) defeats the Village Boss: the camera stops (`scroll_stop_x`, Flink's 0x8723), he falls dead (`MEN3`), the screen shakes and the fanfare (index 21) plays once.
- **Defeat.** Lomax can't be hurt while it dies (`boss_untouchable`, as the Forest Boss). For 161 frames it shakes on its `STAY` frames 0–8 (|(t & 15) − 8|), a spirit every 32 frames (Flink also flashes the screen). Then the men stop, `DEAD`'s frame 1 (and `BOO2`) fly off, its body breaks up (`BOOM`), it counts as defeated, and 100 ticks later the Finish appears in the middle of the screen (Flink drops its crystal and ends the level). After a lost life it is gone (gone.c): the arena has its Finish in the middle of the map, and no wrap.
- **Sounds.** Lomax's EXPLOS2 low (0x58) for Flink's ceiling hit 0x2A, its enemy hit for 0x22, ZAP2 for 0x1A, EXPLOS1 for 0x53.

**The Meta Boss** (31-2: Flink's `Boss` init 0x2948C → 0x2A540 for META, thinks 0x2A5E8 / 0x2A616 / 0x2A656, draw 0x2ACB8, its lightning 0x2AB5A / 0x2AC0A / 0x2AC9C; FLINK.DLL `metaboss.c`). A cloud monster of rock with arms of cloud balls, rising out of the ground in the middle of the 704-px arena.
- **Behind the tiles.** 31-2 is a Priority map (all its tiles over sprites without the priority bit), and the boss's draw gives the bit only to its arms and hands (selector 1): its body and head go behind the play map's tiles, the floor and the dark cloud stumps, its arms in front. Here the arms are the boss's own frame and the body is drawn by a stand-in actor (a copy of the boss's struct, its `B...` frame) on the background plane, at the boss's place on the screen: an actor goes into its plane's buckets, the background plane's under the play plane's tiles, and drawn through `Actor_Draw` it moves with the boss in Lomax+'s in-between frames.
- **Rising.** It waits, unseen, until Lomax's x + 140 is within 16 px of its x (x 254–270 for its placement's 394), then the boss music starts (index 8) and it rises over 64 frames from 140 px under its place (y = home + (0x4000 − sin) × 0x230 >> 16, Flink's table, 1024 a turn), following Lomax already.
- **Its move.** Its face (x + 16) goes toward Lomax's x (kept 90–590): its speed goes toward the distance / 64 px a frame by 0x1000 a frame (0x3000 turning).
- **Lightning.** While Lomax is no more than 60 px above its y, a count (100 at first, then 80–143 frames after each shot) runs down; at 0 the lightning charges at both eyes for 60 frames (`ZAP1`), then a ball is shot from between them (x + 13, y − 79; `ZAP2`, sound 0x3C) toward Lomax's x and the ground (its placement's y + 46: Flink's `Actor_MoveToward`, 1.0 a frame up to 4.0), and strikes there (`ZAP3` played once, sound 0x2D), its hitbox hurting Lomax every frame until it has played. Flink also flashes the screen; not here.
- **Clap and pound.** With the lightning idle, Lomax on the ground and within 12 px of its face, it stops and, one time in 2, claps: `VIER` holds its hands wide 30 frames, brings them together over 23 (sound 0x3E; at that frame `VIER`'s second box, x − 30 … + 59, y − 3 … + 47, hurts Lomax) and opens them over 23; then it waits 60–123 frames. Otherwise it pounds the ground three times (`DREI`, its hands down at frame 11 with sound 0x35 and a shake of 0x23 frames), 20 frames apart, then waits 100–163. Each pound sends up a cloud at x + d − 64, home + 32 (d random 0–127, then + 30–45 each pound, mod 128).
- **The clouds** are Flink's Wolky platforms (see Wolky platforms) rising ever faster (vy from −0.25, 0x200 a frame more): Lomax stands on one, rides it up past the boss's head and jumps or falls onto it. While he is more than 60 px above its y, it doesn't attack and goes under him.
- **Hits.** A stomp on its head (its hitbox, y − 100 … − 89; Flink's `Actor_CheckStompedByPlayer`, as for the other enemies, `flink_stomp_box`): a spirit at y − 100, sound 0x2B, the shake, and for 60 frames it shakes (y ± 4 every other frame) and does nothing else, then waits 100; 120 frames guarded. Not Flink's (its think asks only for the stomp): a thrown object touching its head and body (x − 24 … + 56, y − 100 … − 20: `MB_BODY_*`, BZWE's drawn pixels above the floor; `mboss_struck`, from grab.c's `strike`) and Lomax's spin, roll or thrown helmet reaching them (`player_attack_box`, the helmet turning back as off any actor) are hits too; while it is guarded a thrown object flies through. Touching it doesn't hurt. Flink also brightens three palette colours on the shaking frames; not here. 8 hits.
- **Defeat.** The fanfare (index 21) plays once; for 193 frames it sinks 1 px every other frame (into the floor, behind the tiles), shaking, a spirit every 32 frames with sound 0x1A, and Lomax can't be hurt (`boss_untouchable`). Then its arms break off and fly away (up at 5.0 + (rand & 31) / 8: Flink's break-up 0x1B202 with +3 bit 7 clear, which leaves the selector 2 pieces out) and its body is gone, sound 0x53; it counts as defeated, and 100 ticks later the Finish appears in the middle of the map (Flink drops its crystal, item 0x1A). Defeated, it stays gone for the level visit: the arena then has the Finish from the start.
- **Sounds.** Flink's 0x35, 0x3E, 0x3C, 0x2D, 0x2B, 0x1A and 0x53 (sfx.c).

**The final boss** (24-2: Flink's `Bos1`, init 0x20E86; FLINK.DLL `bos1.c`). A hooded sorcerer, three phases on one actor in the 480-px arena, then the game's end. 24-2 is a Priority map: what Flink draws without the sprite priority bit (actor +3 bit 7, which `Actor_DrawFrame` 0x328C8 puts on the pieces; the sprite lists his draws build have none) goes behind the play map's tiles, the floor and the spikes. Here those are drawn by stand-in actors on the background plane, as the Meta Boss's body: the sparks, the falling sorcerer, the dragon (all but its near segments falling in its leap). The Bos3 dudes, which Flink also draws without it, are drawn at Lomax's layer, in front of the tiles. Flink's camera is the middle 320 px of Lomax's view, its y the view's top + 8.
- **The fireball form.** He starts as a fireball (`HEAD`, with its three sparks: `TAIL`, a spark put at x − 8, y − 8 when its phase's high byte reaches 3, phases + 0x30 a frame) at y 400, below the map, and flies (Actor_MoveToward 0x1B008: 2.0, 0x800) toward Lomax's x + 100 (kept 50 px inside the map) and his home y − 28. Once he moves down within 2 px of that y: sound 0x51, he faces Lomax and forms at his home y (`ZAP` played backwards, 16 frames).
- **Fireballs.** He stands (`STA2`) and touching him hurts. Every 60 frames (the first after 30–61) his point 1 casts a fireball, 5–8 in all: `WARN` 31 frames, then `HEAD` with its sparks (sound 0x3C), flying the way he faces (toward x ± 20000) and to its y − 20, + 10, − 8 or + 20 at 4.0 (0x4000 a frame); touching it hurts; gone outside Flink's mid window. 30 frames after the last:
- **Stomps.** He stands (`STAY`) 100 frames; a stomp on his box (Flink's `Actor_CheckStompedByPlayer`) is a hit, and here any thrown object touching him (`bos1_struck`) or Lomax's spin, roll or thrown helmet (`player_attack_box`) too: a spirit at y − 30, sound 0x2B, the shake, `OUCH` 60 frames. Otherwise touching him hurts. Then, with hits left (3 at first), sound 0x52, `ZAP` forwards (16 frames), and the fireball form again, from his home y − 28 at 4.0 up, toward Lomax's x ± 100: the other side of him.
- **Flying.** After the 3rd stomp: 5 hits, flying away from Lomax across the camera's x + 50 … 270, turning at its edges, at his home y − 100 ± 16 (a new y every 32 frames), 3.0, 0x1000 (`FLY`); touching him does nothing. Every 101 frames (the first after 101) a Bos3 dude drops from `FLY`'s point 2. A hit is his hit flag (+3 bit 6) raised by a thrown object (grab.c's `strike` asks `bos1_struck`; the object breaks, and the next tick counts it). Flink counts it only when the hitter is a dying enemy (its think Enemy_Die's fall 0x1B1AA: a thrown Bos3 dude); here any thrown object counts, and so do Lomax's stomp, spin, roll and thrown helmet on his box, 60 ticks apart (`lomax_hit`, `B1_GUARD`). Sound 0x2B, the shake, `OUCH` until the shake ends, not moving.
- **The Bos3 dudes** (0x21F90, think 0x21FF4): facing Lomax at their spawn (their sprites face right), hopping his way: 1.25 px a frame while rising or in the air, 7 frames on the ground between hops of 2.5 up (gravity 0x2000, 0x1B0E2). Touching one hurts; a stomp stuns it 300 frames (3.0 up), a second stomp or a thrown object kills it. Stunned, it can be picked up: heavy (+6 bit 0), held at +0, +4, thrown in an arc. Gone off the screen (Flink's window 0).
- **The fall.** The 5th hit: `OUCH`, facing left, he falls (+ 0x600 a frame) shrinking from 97% by 0.5 a frame to 50% at the least, toward `OUCH`'s top-left (x − 21, y − 61), behind the tiles (0x21B14). At y 400:
- **The dragon** (0x20F44, think 0x20F96, draw 0x215CC): the camera's x stays (0x8723), 5 hits. Its moves, picked at random (−2, −3, −2, −3, −1, −2, −3, −2 by rand & 7, 0x21086):
  - **hovering with dudes** (3): from y 250 and the camera's x + 160 it rises (vy −1.75, + 0x200 a frame up to 0), sways (x + sin × 10 at 3/1024 of a turn a frame, y + sin × 5 at 9), hovers 13–20 s dropping a Bos3 dude every 128 frames at x + 20, y + 50, then sinks out (to vy 1.75). Only from this move on can a thrown object reach it (+6 bit 3, cleared as the move ends), and a hit counts only while vy is 0; here Lomax's stomp, spin, roll and thrown helmet on its box (`DRAGON_BOX*`, as the thrown objects') count at the same times, 60 ticks apart (`lomax_hit`);
  - **hovering with Booms** (1): the same for 8–11 s, swaying x × 8 at 5, y × 4 at 8, a Boom every 64 frames at x + 10, y + 40: `BHED` with its sparks (sound 0x2F), toward Lomax's x and the ground (his home y) at 3.0, 0x2000, bursting there (`BOOM`, sound 0x35), hurting until it ends;
  - **the leap** (2): from below at y 300 and Lomax's x − 70 (+ 40 when he is right of the camera's x + 160), vx ± 0.5, up at 5.0 falling 1/16 a frame faster, until vy is 9.0. Its depth goes from 7 to 0 as it comes near (one step every 13 frames, after 30).
  - Each frame its depth, x and y go into a ring of 120 entries. It is drawn as 8 segments 8 entries apart, the head (`DHED`, at x − 8) the newest, then 7 `DTAL`, each at its depth's scale ((134 − 8 × depth) %), centred in the 48 × 48 box whose top-left is the entry's point. The hovering depth breathes (|((t >> 3) & 7) − 4|, + the speed in 1/16 px, 3–7; with Booms 5–7). In the leap the far segments (depth over 4) are behind everything, the near ones in front of Lomax, and while it falls in front of the tiles too, hurting Lomax in x … x + 24, y … y + 24 (0x2168C).
- **Defeat.** The fanfare (index 21) plays once; Lomax can't be hurt (`boss_untouchable`). For 320 frames the dragon bounces between the camera's x + 50 … 270 and y + 50 … 70 (2.0, 1.0, swaying), a spirit every 32 frames at x + 20, y + 30 with sound 0x36 and the shake. Then Flink's pad is cleared (here Lomax's input locked), and once he stands Flink presses START for him with message 16 of `HelpTextTable` pending (0x214AC: 0x81A6 = −17): its pause screen shows "WOW!! YOU REALLY SHOWED WAINWRIGHT AND HIS CREW A THING OR TWO!! CONGRATULATIONS!!" (in the game's language) and shuts at START, A, B or C (0x188E8, sound 0x40), and the level ends (Flink: `Level_CompleteMapPoint` 0x2EBC8). Here the message is Lomax's hint box (`hint_box`, hints.c; its text in LEVELS.INI's `[Hints]` as `16.L`, written by the converter's `bos1_params`, `BOS1_MESSAGE`), opened at the same moment and centred in the view, shut by a new press of Jump, Shoot, Ability or Helm (`message_done`). Flink's game stops under its message; here the explosion goes on behind the box until the level's end. The level ends once the box is shut, as the Finish's banner ends it: `g_LastRealSection` + 1, to the map, the fade; after 24 the game ends (Play order). The Finish waits unseen in the middle of the map and appears only if the boss is gone for good on a later visit (gone.c).
- **Not ported:** the screen flashes, START ignored while he falls.
- **Sounds.** Flink's 0x51, 0x52, 0x2B, 0x3C, 0x1A, 0x36, 0x2F, 0x35 (sfx.c); 0x51, 0x52, the dudes' stun 0x48 and landing 0x29 are FM sounds, with no file.

**The Donkey monkey** (28-1: Flink's `Donkey monkey`, init 0x220FC, think 0x22176, its apples 0x22614 / 0x226CC / 0x226F2; FLINK.DLL `donkey.c`). A gorilla on the floor at the top of the tree, at the map's right end. 28-1 is 640 px wide; its Scroll Point (fired by a Generator at x 345) scrolls Flink's camera right to the map's end, and the fight is on that last 320-px screen (x 320–640).
- **Waiting.** It stands (`STAY`) for 100 frames, then jumps once it is on the screen, Flink is no lower than its floor and the camera's x has stopped (0x8738); otherwise it waits another 100. The first jump starts the boss music (index 9). Here it also waits for Lomax to be inside Flink's screen (below): Lomax+'s wider view reaches the map's end sooner and shows the floor to the left of it.
- **`STAY`** has two pieces with hide selectors over its face: Flink's +2 bits 0 and 1 hide them (Actor_DrawFrame 0x328C8), the first shown 8 frames every 60–180 (a blink, table 0x22376), the second shown 11 frames and hidden 7–25 (0x223B4). Here the converter keeps the selectors and FLINK.DLL sets Lomax's actor +0x70 bits 1 and 2.
- **Jumps.** Each jump squats on `JUMP` frames 0–4 (0x80 of a frame a tick), leaves at 5.0 px up, falls 0.125 a frame faster (at most 4.0), lands at its floor and gets up on frames 4–0. A series starts by facing away from the map's right end (left from its width − 160 on), and is 3 jumps across at 1 px a frame one time in 4 and after 3 series in place in a row, otherwise 2 jumps in place (falling on `LAND`). Landing on the screen shakes it 0x13 frames (Flink's counter 0x874E: razorback.c's `flink_quake`), an in-place landing 0x23. After a series it waits 60–123 frames.
- **The apples.** The first in-place landing of a series shakes them out of the canopy: of 6 places across Flink's screen (90 + 40 k px from the monkey's side), one is left out (Rand) and one other is an App2, the rest Falling apples. Each comes down from y 0x3C to 0x50, 1 px a frame, waits for a shake of 0x20 or more (the second landing), then 5 + 10 k frames, and then is itself: the Falling apples (`distance` 1000) shake and fall on Lomax or burst on the floor, the App2 drops, bounces and lies there to be picked up (see Falling apples, Apple (big) and App2).
